Subject: Incident Report Transcription

(FAZ)
--- seg-7eb71a5e5bb1c4c4928f6286 [needs-review] ---
I - Transcribe the b a i x o , for the p r o v i -
d e n c i a s deemed necessary, the occurrence registered in the LRO of the TWR/
VT, service from 28 to 29/03/8*, shift 2130/0930Z - OPR - 1 S TEIXEIRA*
OCCURRENCE: "Approximately at 2159? (local time), I observed a flying object
in the n o r t h e a s t, flying over the continent. The body was generally flattened
and rectangular, almost oval, and entirely enveloped by a strong green light. The light
was intense around the body and left a small but noticeable glowing tail. It was all green.
The speed at which it moved in the n o r t h e a s t from radial 260, when I observed
it, was astounding. It disappeared into the horizon in less than 10 (ten) seconds. I esti-
mate the altitude it was flying at was between 1,500 and 2,000 meters, if not higher.
Several people also observed it in the city during the movement of the telephone tower/
airport and subsequently called this Tower of Control.
At the time, I contacted ACC Brasília, who responded to my call for interception by radar.
Upon observing the area, they reported nothing. It was a lightning-fast movement and the
object had more powerful propulsion, moving along a steady trajectory. It was a splendid
sight. Great illumination around the body and tail visible. No sound of turbines or vibra-
tion was observed, despite the body moving at a relatively high altitude. I transcribe
this fact for the necessary actions. That was the situation. (Below follows a cr o q u i of
the object in the LRO of the TWR.)

Subject: Incident Report Transcription

(FAZ)
--- seg-ff77834ee9fd64a93deee416 [needs-review] ---
I - Below I transcribe, for the necessary investigations, the occurrence registered in the LRO of TWR/VT, service from 28 to 29/03/8*, shift 2130/ O930Z-OPR-1S TEIXEIRA

OCCURRENCE: "Around 2159 (local time), I observed a flying object in the north/south sector, flying over the continent. The body was generally flattened and rectangular, almost oval, entirely enveloped by a strong green light. The light was intense around the body and left a small but noticeable glowing tail. It was all green.

The speed at which it moved in the north/south sector starting from radial 260, when I observed it, was astonishing. It disappeared into the horizon in less than 10 (ten) seconds. I estimate the altitude at which it was flying was between 1,500 meters and 2,000 meters, or even higher. Several people also observed it in the city during its movement from the Tower of Televisions/Airport, and subsequently called this Tower of Control.

At the time of contact, I reached out to ACC Brasilia, who responded to my call for interception by radar. When he observed the area, he noted nothing had been reported. It was a lightning-fast movement, and the object had its own propulsion, moving along a steady trajectory. It was a breathtaking sight. Great illumination around the body and visible tail. No sound of turbines or vibrations was observed, despite the body changing its relative position.

I transcribe these facts for the necessary actions to be taken. This is how it was (Below follows a sketch of the object in the LRO of TWR).
